Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: "chart", "graph", "plot", "data viz", "visualization", "dashboard", "analytics", "visualize data", "categorical colors", "sequential / diverging palette", "stat tile", "sparkline", "heatmap", "legend", "axis", "tooltip", "chart colors", "color by series".

Also use for: permissions ("allow X", "add permission", "move permission to"), env vars ("set X=Y"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: "allow npm commands", "add bq permission to global settings", "move permission to user settings", "set DEBUG=true", "when claude stops show X". - fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.
- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo, defaults to 10m) - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. "check the deploy every 5 minutes", "keep running /babysit-prs"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.
- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.
TRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it "looks like a one-liner" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).

- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). 409		public int PlayerOffset
410		{
411			get
412			{
413				if (!_active)
414				{
415					return 0;
416				}
417				if (_frame >= _data.totalFrames)
418				{
419					return 0;
420				}
421				return _data.playerYOffsets[_frame];
422			}
423		}

426		{

434			}

451		public float RunSpeed
452		{
453			get
454			{
455				if (_type == 4 && _frameState == 4)
456				{
457					return _data.swimSpeed;
458				}
459				if ((_type == 12 || _type == 44 || _type == 49) && _frameState == 4)
460				{
461					return _data.swimSpeed;
462				}
463				if (_type == 12 && _frameState == 2)
464				{
465					return _data.runSpeed + 13.5f;
466				}
467				if (_type == 44 && _frameState == 2)
468				{
469					return _data.runSpeed + 4f;
470				}
471				if (_type == 5 && _frameState == 2)
472				{
473					float num = _fatigue / _fatigueMax;
474					return _data.runSpeed + 4f * (1f - num);
475				}
476				if (_type == 50 && _frameState == 2)
477				{
478					return _data.runSpeed + 2f;
479				}
480				if (_shouldSuperCart)
481				{
482					return SuperCartRunSpeed;
483				}
484				return _data.runSpeed;
485			}
486		}

```
	public static int GetHeightBoost(int MountType)
	{
		if (MountType <= -1 || MountType >= MountID.Count)
		{
			return 0;
		}
		return mounts[MountType].heightBoost;
	}

	public int JumpHeight(float xVelocity)
	{
		int num = _data.jumpHeight;
		switch (_type)
		{
		case 0:
			num += (int)(Math.Abs(xVelocity) / 4f);
			break;
		case 1:
			num += (int)(Math.Abs(xVelocity) / 2.5f);
			break;
		case 4:
		case 49:
			if (_frameState == 4)
			{
				num += 5;
			}
			break;
		}
		if (_shouldSuperCart)
		{
			num = SuperCartJumpHeight;
		}
		return num;
	}

	public float JumpSpeed(float xVelocity)
	{
		float num = _data.jumpSpeed;
		switch (_type)
		{
		case 0:
		case 1:
			num += Math.Abs(xVelocity) / 7f;
			break;
		case 4:
		case 49:
			if (_frameState == 4)
			{
				num += 2.5f;
			}
			break;
		}
		if (_shouldSuperCart)
		{
			num = SuperCartJumpSpeed;
		}
		return num;
	}

	public bool CanFly(Player mountedPlayer)
	{
		if (!_active)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (_type == 54 && ((SelectiveFlyingMountData)_mountSpecificData).allowedToFly)
		{
			return true;
		}
		if (_data.flightTimeMax == 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (_type == 48)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public bool CanHover()
	{
		if (!_active || !_data.usesHover)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (_type == 49)
		{
			return _frameState == 4;
		}
		if (!_data.usesHover)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public IEntitySource GetProjectileSpawnSource(Player mountedPlayer)
	{
		return new EntitySource_Mount(mountedPlayer, Type);
	}

	public void StartAbilityCharge(Player mountedPlayer)
	{
		if (Main.myPlayer == mountedPlayer.whoAmI)
		{
			int type = _type;
			if (type == 9)
			{
				int type2 = 441;
				float num = Main.screenPosition.X + (float)Main.mouseX;
				float num2 = Main.screenPosition.Y + (float)Main.mouseY;
				float ai = num - mountedPlayer.position.X;
				float ai2 = num2 - mountedPlayer.position.Y;
				Projectile.NewProjectile(GetProjectileSpawnSource(mountedPlayer), num, num2, 0f, 0f, type2, 0, 0f, mountedPlayer.whoAmI, ai, ai2);
				_abilityCharging = true;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			int type = _type;
			if (type == 9)
			{
				_abilityCharging = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public void StopAbilityCharge()
	{
		int type = _type;
		if (type == 9 || type == 46)
		{
			_abilityCharging = false;
			_abilityCooldown = _data.abilityCooldown;
			_abilityDuration = _data.abilityDuration;
		}
	}

	public bool CheckBuff(int buffID)
	{
		return _data.buff == buffID;
	}

	public void AbilityRecovery()
	{
		if (_abilityCharging)
		{
			if (_abilityCharge < _data.abilityChargeMax)
			{
				_abilityCharge++;
			}
		}
		else if (_abilityCharge > 0)
		{
			_abilityCharge--;
		}
		if (_abilityCooldown > 0)
		{
			_abilityCooldown--;
		}
		if (_abilityDuration > 0)
		{
			_abilityDuration--;
		}
	}

	public void FatigueRecovery()
	{
		if (_fatigue > 2f)
		{
			_fatigue -= 2f;
		}
		else
		{
			_fatigue = 0f;
		}
	}

	public bool Flight()
	{
		if (_flyTime <= 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		_flyTime--;
		return true;
	}

	public void UpdateAfterEquips(Player mountedPlayer)
	{
		if (!_active)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (_type == 54)
		{
			bool flag = mountedPlayer.wingsLogic > 0;
			((SelectiveFlyingMountData)_mountSpecificData).allowedToFly = flag;
			if (flag && mountedPlayer.empressBrooch)
			{
				_flyTime = mountedPlayer.wingTimeMax;
			}
		}
		if (_type != 55)
		{
			return;
		}
		mountedPlayer.spikedBoots++;
		mountedPlayer.fullRotationOrigin = mountedPlayer.Size / 2f;
		if (mountedPlayer.slideDir >= 1)
		{
			if (MathF.PI / 2f - Math.Abs(mountedPlayer.fullRotation) <= 0.1f)
			{
				mountedPlayer.fullRotation = -MathF.PI / 2f;
			}
			else
			{
				mountedPlayer.fullRotation = mountedPlayer.fullRotation.AngleLerp(-MathF.PI / 2f, 0.5f);
			}
		}
		else if (mountedPlayer.slideDir <= -1)
		{
			if (MathF.PI / 2f - Math.Abs(mountedPlayer.fullRotation) <= 0.1f)
			{
				mountedPlayer.fullRotation = MathF.PI / 2f;
			}
			else
			{
				mountedPlayer.fullRotation = mountedPlayer.fullRotation.AngleLerp(MathF.PI / 2f, 0.5f);
			}
		}
		else if (Math.Abs(mountedPlayer.fullRotation) <= 0.1f)
		{
			mountedPlayer.fullRotation = 0f;
		}
		else
		{
			mountedPlayer.fullRotation = mountedPlayer.fullRotation.AngleLerp(0f, 0.5f);
		}
	}

38	
39	export class Minecart extends Entity {
40	  // 原版坐骑 hitbox：width 20、height 42 + heightBoost 10 = 52（Mount.cs mounts[6]）
41	  w = 20;
42	  h = 52;
43	  mounted = false;          // 玩家骑乘中
44	  dead = false;

46	  texture = DEFAULT_CART_TEXTURE;

48	  superCart = false;

50	  cartFlip = false;
51	  /** 车身倾角（Player.fullRotation = TrackRotation 的 atan2） */
52	  rotation = 0;

54	  gfxOffY = 0;

56	  onTrackNow = false;

70	  fallStart = 0;

76	  get runSpeed(): number { return this.superCart ? SUPER_CART_RUN_SPEED : CART_RUN_SPEED; }
77	  get dashSpeed(): number { return this.superCart ? SUPER_CART_DASH_SPEED : CART_DASH_SPEED; }
78	  get acceleration(): number { return this.superCart ? SUPER_CART_ACCELERATION : CART_ACCELERATION; }

82	    // 骑乘中由 Renderer.drawPlayer 叠画（原版 frontTexture 在玩家之前层）；

84	    if (this.mounted) return;

87	    if (!ctx || !img) return;
88	    const fh = Math.floor(img.height / 3);
89	    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, img.width, fh,
90	      this.x + this.w / 2 - CART_TEXTURE_WIDTH / 2, this.y + this.h / 2 - fh / 2,
91	      img.width, fh);
92	  }
93	
94	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
95	    if (!this.mounted) { this.dead = true; return; }   // 坐骑语义：无骑乘即消亡
96	    const p = game.player;
97	    const st = game.world.store;
98	    const maxRun = this.runSpeed;
99	
100	    // ---- trackBoost 并入（Player.cs:19530-19544：钳 ±maxRunSpeed）----
101	    if (this.trackBoost !== 0) {
102	      this.vx += this.trackBoost;
103	      this.trackBoost = 0;
104	      if (this.vx < -maxRun) this.vx = -maxRun;
105	      else if (this.vx > maxRun) this.vx = maxRun;
106	    }
107	
108	    // ---- 水平输入（Player.cs:19546-19690 Carts 段 1:1）----
109	    // 车只在贴轨（vy==0）时响应加速/反推；runSlowdown+runAcceleration 同帧叠加
110	    const controlLeft = p.inputX < 0, controlRight = p.inputX > 0;
111	    if (controlLeft && this.vx > -maxRun) {
112	      if (this.vy === 0) {
113	        if (this.vx > RUN_SLOWDOWN) this.vx -= RUN_SLOWDOWN;
114	        this.vx -= this.acceleration;
115	      }
116	    } else if (controlRight && this.vx < maxRun) {
117	      if (this.vy === 0) {
118	        if (this.vx < -RUN_SLOWDOWN) this.vx += RUN_SLOWDOWN;
119	        this.vx += this.acceleration;
120	      }
121	    }
122	    // 朝向：仅输入方向与速度同号才转（Player.cs:19553-19561 mount.Cart 门）
123	    if (p.inputX !== 0 && Math.sign(this.vx) === p.inputX) p.facing = p.inputX;
124	
125	    // ---- 跳跃（Player.JumpMovement :20402-20418 + Mount.JumpSpeed/JumpHeight）----
126	    // 起跳需松键重按（releaseJump；autoJump 默认关），续跳按住即可（jump>0 段）
127	    this.jumpSpeed = (this.superCart ? SUPER_CART_JUMP_SPEED : CART_JUMP_SPEED);
128	    if (p.inputJump) {
129	      if (this.jump > 0) {
130	        if (this.vy === 0) this.jump = 0;
131	        else { this.vy = -this.jumpSpeed; this.jump--; }
132	      } else if (!this.prevJump && this.vy === 0 && this.onTrackNow) {
133	        // MountID.Sets.Cart 起跳：位置微抬防立即重贴轨（:20406 position.Y -= 0.001）
134	        this.y -= 0.001;
135	        this.jump = this.jumpHeight;
136	        this.vy = -this.jumpSpeed;
137	      }
138	    } else {
139	      this.jump = 0;
140	    }
141	    this.prevJump = !!p.inputJump;
142	
143	    // ---- 重力（Player.gravity = defaultGravity 0.4；在轨时 TrackCollision 清零 vy）----
144	    if (this.vy === 0) this.fallStart = Math.trunc(this.y / 16);
145	    this.vy += PLAYER_DEFAULT_GRAVITY;
146	
147	    // ---- TrackCollision（Player.cs:27795-27850 1:1）----
148	    this.wasOnTrack = this.onTrackNow;
149	    const prevVy = this.vy;
150	    const rider = {
151	      x: this.x, y: this.y, vx: this.vx, vy: this.vy,
152	      lastBoostX: this.lastBoostX, lastBoostY: this.lastBoostY,
153	    };
154	    const bits = trackCollision(
155	      st, rider, this.w, this.h,
156	      !!p.inputDown, !!p.inputUp, this.fallStart, false,
157	      {
158	        landing: (lx, ly) => {
159	          // MinecartLandingSound（DelegateMethods.Minecart.LandingSound → SoundID.Item53）+ 轮花
160	          game.playSfxFile?.('Item_53', 0.7, lx, ly);
161	          game.spawnParticles(lx, ly + this.h - 4, '#C8B08A', 6, 1.2, { life: 24 });
162	        },
163	      },
164	    );
165	    this.x = rider.x; this.y = rider.y; this.vx = rider.vx; this.vy = rider.vy;
166	    this.lastBoostX = rider.lastBoostX; this.lastBoostY = rider.lastBoostY;
167	
168	    this.onTrackNow = !!(bits & (1 << FLAG_ON_TRACK));
169	    if (this.onTrackNow) {
170	      // 倾角 + gfxOffY（Player.cs:27804-27806：位置前探一步取两轮轨面）
171	      const rot = { v: this.rotation };
172	      this.gfxOffY = trackRotation(st, this.x + this.vx, this.y + this.vy, this.w, this.h,
173	        !!p.inputDown, !!p.inputUp, rot);
174	      this.rotation = rot.v;
175	      if (this.vy === 0) this.cartRampTime = 0;   // Player.cs:26499-26501
176	      if (prevVy > PLAYER_DEFAULT_GRAVITY && this.vy === 0) this.fallStart = Math.trunc(this.y / 16);
177	    } else {
178	      this.rotation = 0;
179	      this.gfxOffY = 0;
180	      // JumpingSound 委托原版为空实现（DelegateMethods.cs:246-248）——离轨不发声
181	    }
182	    if (bits & (1 << FLAG_BOUNCY_BUMPER)) {
183	      if (controlLeft || controlRight) this.cartFlip = !this.cartFlip;
184	      if (this.vx > 0) p.facing = 1;
185	      else if (this.vx < 0) p.facing = -1;
186	      // BumperSound（DelegateMethods.Minecart.BumperSound → SoundID.Item56）
187	      game.playSfxFile?.('Item_56', 0.8, this.x, this.y);
188	    }
189	    if (bits & (1 << FLAG_USED_RAMP)) {
190	      this.cartRampTime = Math.trunc(Math.min(1, Math.abs(this.vx) / this.dashSpeed) * 20);
191	    }
192	    if (bits & (1 << FLAG_HIT_SWITCH)) {
193	      // Player.cs:27927-27931 → Minecart.HitTrackSwitch → Wiring.HitSwitch(磁石位)
194	      const mx = Math.trunc((this.x + this.w / 2 - CART_TEXTURE_WIDTH / 2 + 25) / 16);
195	      const my = Math.trunc((this.y + this.h / 2 + 26) / 16);
196	      game.hitTrackSwitch?.(mx, my);
197	    }
198	    if (bits & (1 << FLAG_BOOST_LEFT)) this.trackBoost -= TRACK_BOOST_DELTA;
199	    if (bits & (1 << FLAG_BOOST_RIGHT)) this.trackBoost += TRACK_BOOST_DELTA;
200	
201	    // ---- 位置积分（原版在 TrackCollision 后由常规碰撞段 position += velocity 完成：
202	    // TrackCollision 只改速度 + 贴轨 Y 快照。轨道非实心——横向撞实心墙即停回退）----
203	    this.x += this.vx;
204	    this.y += this.vy;
205	    if (this.vx !== 0) {
206	      const headX = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2 + Math.sign(this.vx) * this.w / 2) / 16);
207	      const feetY = Math.floor((this.y + this.h - 4) / 16);
208	      if (st.inBounds(headX, feetY) && st.isSolid(headX, feetY)) {
209	        this.x -= this.vx;
210	        this.vx = 0;
211	      }
212	    }
213	
214	    if (Math.abs(this.vx) > 0.1) this.animTick++;
215	    else this.animTick = 0;
216	
217	    // ---- 玩家坐骑位回写（原版玩家 hitbox 即车；此处锁玩家到车位）----
218	    p.x = this.x;
219	    p.y = this.y;
220	    p.vx = this.vx;
221	    p.vy = 0;
222	    p.onGround = this.onTrackNow;
223	    p.fallStartY = null;   // 车上不计摔伤（原版 mount fallStart 语义）
224	
225	    // ---- 矿车撞怪（Player.cs:27225-27296）：速度>4 扩盒命中敌怪 ----
226	    //  盒 = 玩家盒 + 速度扩张（vx<-1 x-15 / vx>1 w+15 / |vx|>10 再 ±10 /
227	    //  vy<-1 y-10 / vy>1 h+10）；敌怪 only（friendly 城镇 NPC 不伤，:27258）；
228	    //  伤害 = DamageVar(25+55×速比)（SuperCart 50+100×速比 :28801-28804；
229	    //  hardMode ×1.5 :28806-28808）；击退 = 10+40×速比；
230	    //  暴击 = max(近/远/魔) 掷骰（:27261-27270）；immune 30t（:27287）；
231	    //  击杀 → HandleSpecialEvent(9) VEHICULAR_MANSLAUGHTER（:27289-27292）
232	    {
233	      const spd = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
234	      if (spd > 4) {
235	        const ratio = spd / p.maxRunSpeed;     // velocity.Length()/maxRunSpeed（动态 stat：buff/配饰乘区）
236	        let rx = this.x, ry = this.y, rw = p.w, rh = p.h;
237	        if (this.vx < -1) rx -= 15;
238	        if (this.vx > 1) rw += 15;
239	        if (this.vx < -10) rx -= 10;
240	        if (this.vx > 10) rw += 10;
241	        if (this.vy < -1) ry -= 10;
242	        if (this.vy > 1) rh += 10;
243	        const dir = this.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1;
244	        const kb = 10 + 40 * ratio;
245	        let base = 25 + 55 * ratio;
246	        if (this.superCart) base = 50 + 100 * ratio;
247	        let dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(base * (1 + (Math.random() * 30 - 15) / 100))); // DamageVar ±15%
248	        if (game.world.flags?.hardMode) dmg = Math.round(dmg * 1.5);
249	        const crit = Math.random() * 100
250	          < Math.max(p.critChance('melee'), p.critChance('ranged'), p.critChance('magic'));
251	        if (crit) dmg = Math.round(dmg * 2);
252	        for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
253	          const e = ent as unknown as {
254	            x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean;
255	            iframes: number; dontTakeDamage?: boolean; vanillaId?: number;
256	            hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks, ap: number, c: boolean, p: number) => boolean;
257	          };
258	          if (e.dead || e.dontTakeDamage) continue;
259	          if (!(rx < e.x + e.w && rx + rw > e.x && ry < e.y + e.h && ry + rh > e.y)) continue;
260	          // 命中：pierce=0 走 hurt 自带免疫门；命中后覆写 30t（原版 immune[i]=30）
261	          if (e.hurt(dmg, dir * kb, 0, game, 0, crit, 0)) {
262	            e.iframes = 30;
263	            if (e.dead) {
264	              game.achievements?.handleSpecialEvent(9,
265	                { statLifeMax: p.maxHp, statManaMax: p.maxMana });
266	            }
267	          }
268	        }
269	      }
270	    }
271	
272	    // 完全脱轨且离轨远处（误用兜底）：无轨重力自然下落由 TileCollision 缺失 → 消亡防穿地
273	    if (!this.onTrackNow && this.vy > 0 && !onTrack(st, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h)
274	      && this.y > game.world.store.h * 16 - 64) {

276	      this.mounted = false;
277	    }
278	  }
279	
280	  /** 轮组动画帧（Mount runningFrameCount 3 / runningFrameDelay 12；静止 standingFrame 0） */
281	  get animFrame(): number {
282	    return Math.abs(this.vx) > 0.1 ? Math.floor(this.animTick / 12) % 3 : 0;
283	  }
284	}

// 宠物物品提取器：Terarria1456/Terraria/Item.cs SetDefaults →
// { itemId: { buff, proj, light } }（light = Main.lightPet[buff]）。
// 判定：case 块内同时出现 `buffType = N` 与 `shoot = N`，且无 `consumable = true`
//（食物=consumable+buffType 无 shoot；武器=shoot 无 buffType）。
// 输出 src/data/vanilla-pets.json；vanity/light 分类取 Main.cs 的
// `vanityPet[N] = true` / `lightPet[N] = true` 赋值表。
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const ROOT = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..');
const SRC = join(ROOT, 'Terarria1456', 'Terraria', 'Item.cs');
const MAIN = join(ROOT, 'Terarria1456', 'Terraria', 'Main.cs');
const OUT = join(ROOT, 'game', 'src', 'data', 'vanilla-pets.json');

const itemSrc = readFileSync(SRC, 'utf8');
const mainSrc = readFileSync(MAIN, 'utf8');

// ---- Main.cs vanityPet/lightPet 布尔表 ----
const grabBoolTable = (name) => {
  const set = new Set();
  const re = new RegExp(`(?:vanityPet|lightPet)\\[${name}\\]`); // 不抓表本身，见下方逐条
  void re;
  return set;
};
// 实际赋值形如 `vanityPet[N] = true;` 或 `lightPet[N] = true;`
const vanityPet = new Set();
const lightPet = new Set();
for (const m of mainSrc.matchAll(/vanityPet\[(\d+)\] = true;/g)) vanityPet.add(+m[1]);
for (const m of mainSrc.matchAll(/lightPet\[(\d+)\] = true;/g)) lightPet.add(+m[1]);

// ---- Item.cs SetDefaults switch 逐 case 解析 ----
// switch 起止：`switch (type)`（SetDefaults 内第一个）到文件尾的 case 区。
// 用行扫描维护 case 标签栈（case N:/case N:/default: 共享块），块内收集赋值。
const lines = itemSrc.split('\n');
let pets = {};
let curCases = [];
let block = null; // { buff, shoot, consumable }
const flush = () => {
  if (block && curCases.length && block.buff != null && block.shoot != null && !block.consumable) {
    for (const id of curCases) {
      pets[id] = { buff: block.buff, proj: block.shoot, light: lightPet.has(block.buff) ? 1 : 0 };
    }
  }
  block = null;
};
const startBlock = () => {
  flush();
  block = { buff: null, shoot: null, consumable: false };
};
for (const line of lines) {
  const t = line.trim();
  const cm = t.match(/^case (\d+):$/);
  if (cm) {
    if (!block) startBlock();
    curCases.push(+cm[1]);
    continue;
  }
  if (t === 'default:') {
    flush();

			mountedPlayer.position.Y -= mountedPlayer.height;
		}
	}

	public void SetMount(int m, Player mountedPlayer)
	{
		if (_type == m || m <= -1 || m >= MountID.Count || (m == 5 && mountedPlayer.wet))
		{
			return;
		}
		if (_active)
		{
			mountedPlayer.ClearBuff(_data.buff);
			if (AnyTrackRider)
			{
				mountedPlayer.cartFlip = false;
				mountedPlayer.lastBoost = Vector2.Zero;
			}
			mountedPlayer.fullRotation = 0f;
			mountedPlayer.fullRotationOrigin = Vector2.Zero;
			_mountSpecificData = null;
		}
		else
		{
			_active = true;
		}
		_flyTime = 0;
		_type = m;
		_data = mounts[m];
		_fatigueMax = _data.fatigueMax;
		if (!mountedPlayer.isDisplayDollOrInanimate && mountedPlayer.whoAmI == Main.myPlayer)
		{
			NetMessage.SendData(13, -1, -1, null, mountedPlayer.whoAmI);
		}
		mountedPlayer.AddBuff(_data.buff, 3600);
		_flipDraw = false;
		if (_type == 44)
		{
			mountedPlayer.velocity *= 0.2f;
			mountedPlayer.dash = 0;
			mountedPlayer.dashType = 0;
			mountedPlayer.dashDelay = 0;
			mountedPlayer.dashTime = 0;
		}
		if (_type == 9 && _abilityCooldown < 20)
		{
			_abilityCooldown = 20;
		}
		if (_type == 46 && _abilityCooldown < 40)
		{
			_abilityCooldown = 40;
		}
		MountDelegatesData.OverrideSizeMethod playerSize = _data.delegations.PlayerSize;
		if (playerSize != null && playerSize(mountedPlayer, out var size) && size.HasValue)
		{
			Vector2 value = size.Value;
			Vector2 bottom = mountedPlayer.Bottom;
			mountedPlayer.position = mountedPlayer.Bottom;
			for (int i = 0; i < mountedPlayer.shadowPos.Length; i++)
			{
				mountedPlayer.shadowPos[i].X += mountedPlayer.width / 2;
				mountedPlayer.shadowPos[i].Y += mountedPlayer.height;
			}
			mountedPlayer.width = (int)value.X;
			mountedPlayer.height = (int)value.Y;
			mountedPlayer.position = new Vector2(bottom.X - value.X / 2f, bottom.Y - (float)mountedPlayer.height);
			for (int j = 0; j < mountedPlayer.shadowPos.Length; j++)
			{
				mountedPlayer.shadowPos[j].X -= mountedPlayer.width / 2;
				mountedPlayer.shadowPos[j].Y -= mountedPlayer.height;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			mountedPlayer.position.Y += mountedPlayer.height;
			for (int k = 0; k < mountedPlayer.shadowPos.Length; k++)
			{
				mountedPlayer.shadowPos[k].Y += mountedPlayer.height;
			}
			mountedPlayer.height = 42 + _data.heightBoost;
			mountedPlayer.position.Y -= mountedPlayer.height;
			for (int l = 0; l < mountedPlayer.shadowPos.Length; l++)
			{
				mountedPlayer.shadowPos[l].Y -= mountedPlayer.height;
			}
		}
		mountedPlayer.ResetAdvancedShadows();
		if (_type == 7 || _type == 8)
		{
			mountedPlayer.fullRotationOrigin = new Vector2(mountedPlayer.width / 2, mountedPlayer.height / 2);
		}
		int type = _type;
		if ((uint)(type - 62) <= 1u)
		{
			SoundEngine.PlaySound(SoundID.PalChillet, mountedPlayer.Center);
		}
		if (_type == 8)
		{
			_mountSpecificData = new DrillMountData();
		}
		if (_type == 35)
		{
			_mountSpecificData = new ExtraFrameMountData();
		}
		if (_type == 54)
		{
			_mountSpecificData = new SelectiveFlyingMountData();
		}
		FinalizeMountData(m, mountedPlayer);
		DoSpawnDust(mountedPlayer, isDismounting: false);
		if (_type == 38 && mountedPlayer.whoAmI == Main.myPlayer)
		{
			AchievementsHelper.NotifyProgressionEvent(32);
		}
	}

	public void FinalizeMountData(int m, Player mountedPlayer)
	{
		if (_type == 8 && mountedPlayer.isDisplayDollOrInanimate)

			float diodeRotation = 0f;
			if (mountedPlayer.direction == -1)
			{
				diodeRotation = MathF.PI;
			}
			((DrillMountData)_mountSpecificData).diodeRotation = diodeRotation;
		}
	}

// 原版素材接入：按白名单从 terraria-assets 拷贝 PNG + 提取 TEdit 元数据 → public/sprites/vanilla.json
// 原版 Tiles 表无 .meta（不同于 Maples Unity 项目），按 textureGrid+frameGap 网格寻址：
//   帧 (col,row) 位于 (col*(gridX+gapX), row*(gridY+gapY))，帧尺寸 = textureGrid
// 用法：node scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, copyFileSync, mkdirSync, existsSync, readdirSync, unlinkSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const ROOT = join(here, '..');
const ASSETS = join(ROOT, '..', 'terraria-assets', 'Images');
const TEDIT_DATA = join(ROOT, '..', 'Terraria-Map-Editor', 'src', 'TEdit.Terraria', 'Data');
const OUT_DIR = join(ROOT, 'public', 'sprites', 'vanilla');
const OUT_JSON = join(ROOT, 'public', 'sprites', 'vanilla.json');
const whitelist = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(here, 'vanilla-whitelist.json'), 'utf8'));

/** 读 PNG IHDR（8 字节签名 + 4 长度 + 4 类型后即宽高，各 4 字节大端） */
function pngSize(file) {
  const b = readFileSync(file);
  return { w: b.readUInt32BE(16), h: b.readUInt32BE(20) };
}

const tiles = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(TEDIT_DATA, 'tiles.json'), 'utf8'));
const items = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(TEDIT_DATA, 'items.json'), 'utf8'));
const walls = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(TEDIT_DATA, 'walls.json'), 'utf8'));
const tileById = new Map(tiles.map((t) => [t.id, t]));
const itemById = new Map(items.map((i) => [i.id, i]));
const wallById = new Map(walls.map((w) => [w.id, w]));

mkdirSync(OUT_DIR, { recursive: true });

// 杂项单图素材（呼吸气泡等，非表驱动）+ 树木专用贴图（TEdit WorldRenderXna 逻辑）

  'Bubble.png',

  ...Array.from({ length: 32 }, (_, i) => `Tree_Branches_${i}.png`),

// 注意：Unity 精灵 rect 的 y 轴原点在【左下】，Canvas 在【左上】，取用时要翻转。

import resourcesJson from '../../public/sprites/resources.json';
import vanillaJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla.json';

  ensureVImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
    const hit = this.vimages.get(file);
    if (hit) return hit;
    if (this._iconPending.has(file)) return null;
    this._iconPending.add(file);
    const im = new Image();
    im.onload = () => {
      this.vimages.set(file, im);
      this._iconPending.delete(file);
      this.onVImageLoaded?.(file);  // 晚到的表 → ChunkCache 重烘焙
    };
    im.onerror = () => { this._iconPending.delete(file); };
    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
    return null;
  }
  /** 后台预取全部物品图标(保留兼容入口;预载流程请用 awaited 的 preloadIcons) */
  prefetchIcons(): void {
    void this.preloadIcons();
  }

  private _iconsPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;
  /** 预载全部物品图标素材。图集化后清单 = 去重后的 ~3 张 Item_Atlas_k.png
   *  (此前 6059 张单体逐张请求);缓存 Promise——并发 await 的调用者
   *  都会等到同一批加载完成(此前旗标早退会让第二个调用者拿到假完成) */
  preloadIcons(onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
    if (this._iconsPromise) return this._iconsPromise;
    const icons = [...new Set(Object.values(this.vanilla.items).map((m) => m.icon))];
    let done = 0;
    this._iconsPromise = Promise.all(icons.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
      if (this.vimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / icons.length); return resolve(); }
      const im = new Image();

//    离开后从 30s 倒计时自然消退）。
//  - 效果聚合按 UpdateBuffs L9640+ 逐 case，数值一律取 1456：
//      Regeneration(2): lifeRegen += 4（=2 HP/s）
//      Swiftness(3): moveSpeed += 0.25
//      Ironskin(5): statDefense += 8
//      Thorns(14): thorns = 1 → 反弹接触伤害全额（cap 1000，StrikeNPC 侧 L30940-30944）
//      PotionSickness(21): 封锁治疗药水
//      Honey(48): lifeRegenTime += 2、lifeRegen += 2（=1 HP/s）；lifeRegen<0 时 +4 对冲
//      （debuff 系统未移植，对冲分支暂缺）；授予来源 = 浸蜜（Player.cs:27436
//      AddBuff(48,1800)）/ 蜂蜜史莱姆接触（:30904）/ 蜂窝饰品受击（:37905 AddBuff(48,300)）
//      Campfire(87)：原版为 SceneMetrics.HasCampfire 光环 lifeRegen++（:18990）——
//      本仓库沿用 Game 每 20tick 扫描写入的持续小时长 buff 表达
// 名称/描述走原版 l10n：BuffName.<Internal> + 自有 Mods.SandboxWorld.Buff.* 描述
import { Lang } from '../i18n/Lang';

export enum BuffType {
  Agility = 0,    // 敏捷：移速 +25% → Swiftness(3)
  Ironskin = 1,   // 铁皮：防御 +8 → Ironskin(5)
  Resistance = 2, // 耐药性：立即回 80 HP，期间禁用治疗药水（60s）→ PotionSickness(21)
  Thorns = 3,     // 荆棘：反弹接触伤害全额（cap 1000）→ Thorns(14)
  Regen = 4,      // 恢复：2 HP/s → Regeneration(2)
  Campfire = 5,   // 篝火：范围光环(由附近篝火/心灯实体驱动,Game 每帧续期) → Campfire(87)
  Honey = 6,      // 蜂蜜：1 HP/s（浸蜜授予，30s）→ Honey(48)
  OnFire = 7,     // 着火(24)：4 HP/s（lifeRegen-8,Player.cs:18793）；入水熄灭（:27426）
  Burning = 8,    // 燃烧(67)：30 HP/s（lifeRegen-60）+ 移速减半；站上陨石/狱石授予（ApplyTouchDamage）
  Bleeding = 9,   // 流血(30)：清自然恢复计时 lifeRegenTime=0（:18998,无直接 DoT）

/** 环境 DoT 表（NaturalLifeRegen :18779-18911 的 onFire→burned→suffocating 顺序，
 *  速率 = lifeRegen 负项 / 2 = HP/s）。每个 DoT 结算前先清零正 lifeRegen——
 *  恢复类 buff 不能抵消 DoT（tick 内消费） */
const ENV_DOTS: ReadonlyArray<readonly [BuffType, number]> = [
  [BuffType.OnFire, -4],      // lifeRegen-8（:18793，vampireSeed 分支未移植）
  [BuffType.Burning, -30],    // lifeRegen-60（:18862，另 moveSpeed×0.5 在 moveSpeedMult）
  [BuffType.Suffocation, -20], // lifeRegen-40（:18889）
  [BuffType.Poisoned, -2],    // lifeRegen-4 → 2 HP/s（Player.cs:18795 poisoned 分支）
];

export class BuffState {
  active = new Map<BuffType, number>(); // 剩余秒（≈buffTime/60）
  campfireAccum = 0;
  /** 本仓已实装的 debuff 集（Main.debuff 表 ∩ BuffType：环境伤害/控速/DoT 族——
   *  魔力眩晕 94 亦在原版 debuff 表，护士可清） */
  static readonly DEBUFFS = new Set<BuffType>([
    BuffType.OnFire, BuffType.Burning, BuffType.Bleeding, BuffType.Suffocation,
    BuffType.Chilled, BuffType.Weak, BuffType.Slow, BuffType.ManaSickness, BuffType.Poisoned,
  ]);
  /** 活跃 debuff 计数（Main.GetNurseHealCost :39466-39472 的 +100/个 段：
   *  buffTime>60 且 Main.debuff；NurseCannotRemoveDebuff 排除表未实装，登记） */
  debuffCount(minSeconds = 0): number {
    let n = 0;
    for (const [bt, sec] of this.active) {
      if (BuffState.DEBUFFS.has(bt) && sec > minSeconds) n++;
    }
    return n;
  }
  /** 清全部 debuff（护士治疗 NPCChatText_DoNurseHeal 语义） */
  clearDebuffs(): void {
    for (const bt of [...this.active.keys()]) {
      if (BuffState.DEBUFFS.has(bt)) this.active.delete(bt);
    }
  }
  /** 狼人回复独立累加器（:11496 lifeRegen++ 恒 0.5 HP/s，与篝火强度/节拍无关） */
  werewolfAccum = 0;
  lanternAccum = 0;
  /** 当前篝火光环强度(Game 每 20tick 扫描写入:篝火+1,心灯额外+1) */
  campfireHeal = 1;
  /** 心灯(lifeRegen+2 = 1 HP/s,Player.cs:12247):Game 扫描写入 0/1 */
  lanternHeal = 0;
  /** AddBuff 语义（Player.cs:5138-5150）：同类取 max 不缩短——浸入类 buff 可每帧无脑续期 */
  apply(type: BuffType, durationSec: number) {
    const cur = this.active.get(type);
    if (cur === undefined || cur < durationSec) this.active.set(type, durationSec);
  }
  has(type: BuffType): boolean {
    return this.active.has(type);
  }
  remove(type: BuffType) {
    this.active.delete(type);
  }
  /** 直写剩余秒（原版 `buffTime[i] = n` 赋值语义，可缩短——区别于 apply 的
   *  AddBuff max 合并）。消费方：玩家微光穿落 buffTime=6t 刷新（Player.cs:11416） */
  set(type: BuffType, durationSec: number) {
    this.active.set(type, durationSec);
  }

'Mount_Bunny.png',\n  PigronMount: () => 'Mount_Pigron.png',\n  SlimeMount: () => 'Mount_Slime.png',\n  TurtleMount: () => 'Mount_Turtle.png',\n  UnicornMount: () => 'Mount_Unicorn.png',\n  BasiliskMount: () => 'Mount_Basilisk.png',\n  BeeMount: (i) => (i === 1 ? 'Mount_BeeWings.png' : 'Mount_Bee.png'),\n  UfoMount: (i) => (i === 1 ? 'Mount_UFOGlow.png' : 'Mount_UFO.png'),\n  DrillMount: ['Mount_DrillRing.png', 'Mount_DrillSeat.png', 'Mount_DrillDiode.png',\n    'Mount_Glow_DrillRing.png', 'Mount_Glow_DrillSeat.png', 'Mount_Glow_DrillDiode.png'],\n  ScutlixMount: ['Mount_Scutlix.png', 'Mount_ScutlixEyes.png', 'Mount_ScutlixEyeGlow.png'],\n  CuteFishronMount: (i) => (i === 1 ? 'Mount_MinecartMechGlow.png' : 'Mount_MinecartMech.png'),\n  MinecartWoodMount: () => 'Mount_MinecartWood.png',\n  DesertMinecartMount: () => 'Mount_MinecartDesert.png',\n  FishMinecartMount: () => 'Mount_MinecartMineCarp.png',\n  Extra: (i) => `Extra_${i}.png`,\n};\nconst texOf = (expr) => {\n  const m = expr.match(/TextureAssets\\.(\\w+)\\s*(?:\\[(\\d+)\\])?/);\n  if (!m) return null;\n  const null;\n};\n\n// ---- 单块解析：`mountData.X = 值;` + playerYOffsets 循环填充 + delta + 贴图 ----\nconst NUM_FIELDS = ['spawnDust', 'buff', 'heightBoost', 'flightTimeMax', 'extraFall',\n  'jumpHeight', 'abilityChargeMax', 'abilityDuration', 'abilityCooldown',\n  'walkingGraceTimeMax', 'totalFrames', 'playerHeadOffset', 'xOffset', 'yOffset',\n  'bodyFrame', 'playerXOffset', 'standingFrameCount', 'standingFrameDelay', 'standingFrameStart',\n  'runningFrameCount', 'runningFrameDelay', 'runningFrameStart',\n  'flyingFrameCount', 'flyingFrameDelay', 'flyingFrameStart',\n  'inAirFrameCount', 'inAirFrameDelay', 'inAirFrameStart',\n  'idleFrameCount', 'idleFrameDelay', 'idleFrameStart',\n  'swimFrameCount', 'swimFrameDelay', 'swimFrameStart',\n  'dashingFrameCount', 'dashingFrameDelay', 'dashingFrameStart'];\nconst FLOAT_FIELDS = ['runSpeed', 'dashSpeed', 'swimSpeed', 'acceleration', 'jumpSpeed',\n  'fallDamage', 'fatigueMax'];\nconst BOOL_FIELDS = ['usesHover', 'constantJump', 'blockExtraJumps', 'Minecart',\n  'CanRideMinecartTracks', 'CanUseWings', 'idleFrameLoop', 'spawnDustNoGravity',\n  'dismountsOnItemUse', 'emitsLight'];\n\nfunction parseBlock(body, prefix, extra = {}) {\n  const d = { ...extra };\n  const lines = body.split('\\n');\n  const rel = (f) => `${prefix}.${f}`;\n  let yBase = null;                 // playerYOffsets 循环基值\n  const yDelta = new Map();         // 帧索引 → 增量\n  for (let li = 0; li < lines.length; li++) {\n    const t = lines[li].trim();\n    for (const f of NUM_FIELDS) {\n      const m = t.match(new RegExp(`^${rel(f)} = (-?\\\\d+);?$`));\n      if (m) d[f] = +m[1];\n    }\n    for (const f of FLOAT_FIELDS) {\n      const m = t.match(new RegExp(`^${rel(f)} = (-?[\\\\d.]+)f?;?$`));\n      if (m) d[f] = +m[1];\n    }\n    for (const f of BOOL_FIELDS) {\n      const m = t.match(new RegExp(`^${rel(f)} = (true|false);$`));\n      if (m) d[f] = m[1] === 'true';\n    }\n    // playerYOffsets 循环填充：array[i] = <num> - vertOff + pVertOff\n    const fill = t.match(/^array\\[i\\] = (-?\\d+)(?: - (\\w+))?(?: \\+ (\\w+))?;/);\n    if (fill) yBase = { v: +fill[1], minus: fill[2] ?? null, plus: fill[3] ?? null };\n    const plus = t.match(/^array\\[(\\w+)\\] \\+= (-?\\d+);$/);\n    if (plus) {\n      const k = plus[1] === 'array.Length - 1' ? -1 : +plus[1];\n      yDelta.set(k, (yDelta.get(k) ?? 0) + (+plus[2]));\n    }\n    const set = t.match(/^array\\[(\\d+)\\] -= (-?\\d+);$/);\n    if (set) yDelta.set(+set[1], (yDelta.get(+set[1]) ?? 0) - (+set[2]));\n    // 贴图四层\n    for (const slot of ['backTexture', (TextureAssets\\\\.[^;]+?);?$`));\n        if (m2 && !m2[1].includes('Asset<Texture2D>.Empty')) d[slot] = m2[1];\n      }\n    }\n    // 发光色\n    const lc = t.match(new RegExp(`^${rel('lightColor')} = new Vector3\\\\((-?[\\\\d.]+)f, *(-?[\\\\d.]+)f, *(-?[\\\\d.]+)f\\\\);$`));\n    if (lc) d.lightColor = [+lc[1], +lc[2], +lc[3]];\n  }\n  // 展开贴图 → 文件名（缺素材字段保留原表达式供登记）\n  for (const slot of ['backTexture', 'backTextureGlow', 'backTextureExtra', 'frontTexture', 'frontTextureGlow', 'frontTextureExtra']) {\n    if (typeof d[slot] === 'string') d[slot] = texOf(d[slot]) ?? d[slot];\n  }\n  // playerYOffsets 定稿\n  if (yBase !== null && d.totalFrames) {\n    const n = d.totalFrames;\n    const off = yBase.minus === 'verticalOffset' ? (extra.verticalOffset ?? 0) : 0;\n    const add = yBase.plus === 'playerVerticalOffset' ? (extra.playerVerticalOffset ?? 0) : 0;\n    d.playerYOffsets = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) =>\n      yBase.v - off + add + (yDelta.get(i) ?? 0));\n    if (yDelta.has(-1)) d.playerYOffsets[n - 1] += yDelta.get(-1);\n  }\n  if (d.yOffset !== undefined && extra.verticalOffset) d.yOffset += extra.verticalOffset;\n  return d;\n}\n\n// ---- Initialize() 逐 mounts[N] 切块 ----\nconst mounts = {};\nconst blocks = [];\n{\n  const re = /mounts\\[(\\d+)\\] = mountData;/g;\n  let m;\n  const marks = [];\n  while ((m = re.exec(initBody))) marks.push({ id: +m[1], at: m.index });\n  for (let i = 0; i < marks.length; i++) {\n    const end = i + 1 < marks.length ? marks[i + 1].at : initBody.length;\n    const seg = initBody.slice(marks[i].at, end);\n    // SetAs* 助手调用：展开助手体后叠加本段覆写\n    const call = seg.match(/SetAs(Minecart|Horse|RollerSkate|Chillet)\\(([^;]*)\\);/);\n    let data = {};\n    if (call) {\n      const args = call[2].split(',').map((s) => s.trim());\n      // 参数：Minecart(buff, tex[, vertOff[, pVertOff]]) / 其余(buff, tex)\n      const vert = call[1] === 'Minecart' ? +(args[2] ?? 0) : 0;\n      const pVert = call[1] === 'Minecart' ? +(args[3] ?? 0) : 0;\n      data = parseBlock(helpers[`SetAs${call[1]}`], 'newMount',\n        { verticalOffset: vert, playerVerticalOffset: pVert });\n      if (call[1] === 'Minecart') { data.verticalOffset = vert; data.playerVerticalOffset = pVert; }\n    }\n    const tail = seg.slice(seg.indexOf('mounts['));\n    Object.assign(data, parseBlock(tail, 'mountData'));\n    // 助手段内 texture 形参（SetAsHorse 传入 textureAsset）\n    if (call && data.backTexture === undefined && /backTexture = texture;/.test(helpers[`SetAs${call[1]}`])) {\n      const t = args[1].match(/TextureAssets\\.(\\w+)\\s*(?:\\[(\\d+)\\])?/);\n      if (t) data.backTexture = texOf(args[1]) ?? args[1];\n    }\n    // 后续覆盖特例：mount 39 yOffset -= 2 / frontTextureExtra；mount 38 backTexture=frontTexture\n    const sub = tail.match(/mountData\\.yOffset -= (\\d+);/);\n    if (sub) data.yOffset = (data.yOffset ?? 0) - +sub[1];\n    const btf = tail.match(/mountData\\.backTexture = mountData\\.frontTexture;/);\n    if (btf) data.backTexture = data.frontTexture ?? data.backTexture;\n    mounts[marks[i].id] = data;\n    blocks.push(marks[i].id);\n  }\n}\n// mounts[6]（默认矿车）在 Initialize 内为显式块；SetAsMinecart 族已含 Minecart=true\n\n// ---- Item.cs：case 块 → mountType ----\nconst itemMount = {};\n{\n  let cur = null;\n  let mountAssign = null;\n  const flush = () => {\n    if (cur != null && mountAssign != null) {\n      for (const id of cur) itemMount[id] = mountAssign(id);\n    }\n    cur = null; mountAssign = null;\n  };\n  for (const line of itemSrc.split('\\n')) {\n    const t = line.trim();\n    const cm = t.match(/^case (\\d+):$/);\n    if (cm) { if (cur == null) cur = []; cur.push(+cm[1]); continue; }\n    if (t === 'default:') { flush(); continue; }\n    const mm = t.match(/^mountType = (\\d+);$/);\n    if (mm) { mountAssign = () => +mm[1]; flush(); continue; }\n    const mf = t.match(/^mountType = (\\d+) \\+ \\(type - (\\d+)\\);$/);\n    if (mf) { const base = +mf[1], anchor = +mf[2]; mountAssign = (id) => base + (id - anchor); flush(); continue; }\n    const mf2 = t.match(/^mountType = (\\d+) \\+ type - (\\d+);$/);\n    if (mf2) { const base = +mf[1], anchor = +mf[2]; mountAssign = (id) => base + id - anchor; flush(); continue; }\n  }\n}\n\n// ---- BuffID.cs：Sets.MountType ----\nconst buffMount = {};\n{\n  const line = buffIdSrc.match(/public static int\\[\\] MountType = Factory\\.CreateIntSet\\(([^;]*)\\);/)?.[1] ?? '';\n  const args = [...line.matchAll(/-?\\d+/g)].map((a) => +a[0]);\n  args.shift();                       // 首参 = 缺省值 -1\n  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < args.length; i += 2) buffMount[args[i]] = args[i + 1];\n}\n\n// ---- 贴图可得性登记 ----\nconst textureOk = {};\nconst texAvailable = (file) => typeof file === 'string' && /\\.png$/.test(file) && existsSync(join(IMG, file));\nfor (const [id, d] of Object.entries(mounts)) {\n  const layers = [d.backTexture, d.frontTexture, d.backTextureExtra, d.frontTextureExtra,\n    d.backTextureGlow, d.frontTextureGlow].filter((f) => f !== undefined);\n  const main = [d.backTexture, d.frontTexture].filter((f) => f !== undefined);\n  textureOk[id] = main.length > 0 && main.every(texAvailable);\n  // 全层缺素材的层剔除（不进管线），保留登记信息\n  for (const slot of ['backTexture', 'frontTexture', 'backTextureExtra', 'frontTextureExtra',\n    'backTextureGlow', 'frontTextureGlow']) {\n    if (typeof d[slot] === 'string' && !texAvailable(d[slot])) delete d[slot];\n  }\n  if (d.playerYOffsets) d.playerYOffsets = d.playerYOffsets.map((v) => +v.toFixed(2));\n}\n\nconst out = { count: mountCount, names, sets, mounts, itemMount, buffMount, textureOk };\nwriteFileSync(OUT, JSON.stringify(out, null, 1) + '\\n');\nconst okN = Object.values(textureOk).filter(Boolean).length;\nconst total = Object.keys(mounts).length;\nconsole.log(`vanilla-mounts.json: ${total}/${mountCount} mounts, ${okN} 有主贴图, ` +\n  `${Object.keys(itemMount).length} 召唤物品, ${Object.keys(buffMount).length} 坐骑 buff`);\nconst missing = Object.entries(mounts)\n  .filter(([id]) => !textureOk[id])\n  .map(([id]) => `${id}:${names[id] ?? '?'}`);\nif (missing.length) console.log(`贴图不可得（色块近似渲染登记）: ${missing.join(' ')}`);\n"
}

  /** 本帧弹出的 DoT 分块伤害合计（HurtLifeRegen 语义,Player 消费后弹出飘字并清零） */
  private dotChunkOut = 0;
  consumeDotChunk(): number {
    const c = this.dotChunkOut;
    this.dotChunkOut = 0;
    return c;
  }
  /** 每逻辑帧（dt 秒）。返回本帧生命净变化（正=回复,负=持续伤害/DoT,无视无敌帧与防御） */
  tick(dt: number): number {
    let heal = 0;
    for (const [t, time] of [...this.active]) {
      const left = time - dt;
      if (left <= 0) this.active.delete(t);
      else this.active.set(t, left);
    }
    // ---- UpdateBuffs 正项（Player.cs:9640+）：Regeneration lifeRegen+=4（2 HP/s）、
    //      Honey :18955 lifeRegen+=2（1 HP/s）、狼人 :11496 lifeRegen+=1（0.5 HP/s）。
    //      注意原版这些都在 NaturalLifeRegen 的 DoT 段（:18779+）**之前**求值——
    //      DoT 的 if(lifeRegen>0) lifeRegen=0 会把它们整池清零（见下）----
    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Regen, 2, dt);
    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Honey, 1, dt);
    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Werewolf)) {
      // 狼人 lifeRegen++（:11496）= 0.5 HP/s → 每 2 秒恒 1 HP（独立累加器，勿与篝火
      // 共用 campfireAccum/campfireHeal——曾误挂致篝火不在时狼人回复随强度归零/错峰）
      this.werewolfAccum += dt;
      if (this.werewolfAccum >= 2) { this.werewolfAccum -= 2; heal += 1; }
    }
    // ---- DoT 段（NaturalLifeRegen :18779-18911）：每个 DoT 先 if(lifeRegen>0)
    //      lifeRegen=0 再累加负项——恢复类 buff **不能**抵消 OnFire/Burning 等
    //      （曾直接相加，恢复池被 DoT 按净额吃掉）。分块弹出（DOT_CHUNK），
    //      净伤害记入 dotChunkOut 供 HurtLifeRegen 飘字消费 ----
    let dots = 0;
    for (const [t, hpPerSec] of ENV_DOTS) {
      if (!this.active.has(t)) continue;
      if (heal > 0) heal = 0;                       // :18781 等 if (lifeRegen > 0) lifeRegen = 0
      const d = this.rateAccum(t, hpPerSec, dt);
      dots += d;
      heal += d;
    }
    if (dots < 0) this.dotChunkOut += -dots;

mount: MountInstance
get ridingMount(): boolean { return this.mount.active && !this.mount.isCart; }
```

Height: vanilla mount raises player height by heightBoost. In our engine, changing player h dynamically affects collision everywhere. Cart handled it with a separate entity + rideBox. For mounts, I'll implement `mountRideH` = 42 + heightBoost and apply in collision via a similar rideBox approach... that's invasive. Simpler: keep player box as-is (20×42) and only adjust the visual offset (player drawn at mount offset). But then tall mounts (heightBoost 34 → h 76) would clip into ceilings less than vanilla.

Compromise: implement `rideBoxH` = 42 + heightBoost used in:
- canDismount space check (1:1 needed)
- hurtTiles/drown sampling? vanilla uses the mounted hitbox.
- Draw offset: playerYOffsets per frame.

For movement collision we keep the player's own box (20×42) — a deviation, registered. Actually wait — mounting in vanilla is bottom-anchored: position.Y += height then height = 42+boost then Y -= height. So the player's bottom stays, top rises. If a mount is 76 tall and player is under a 3-tile ceiling, CanMount fails (CanFitInSpaceWithSize). That's the gate. During riding, the taller box collides with ceilings. Implementing full dynamic-height collision is risky; I'll do:
- CanMount: check free space for 20×(42+boost) box (1:1).
- While riding: use rideH in vertical collision head-bump check? Let me see how Player.ts does collision — there's likely a moveAndCollide helper. Let me check quickly.

  /** 冲刺头部将撞墙探测（DashMovement :21132-21137）：身前 dir×半宽+2px 的
   *  头部点（gravDir 对应侧 h/2−2）与身体中点格任一 SolidOrSlopedTile → true
   *  （触发帧 velocity.X /= 2）。探测点恒在身体行内——对整砖随后必撞停（原版同），
   *  实际差异面 = 半砖/坡面（SolidOrSloped 含之） */
  dashWallAhead(dir: number): boolean {
    const st = this.lastStore;
    if (!st) return false;   // fixedUpdate 之前调用：无缓存世界视为不撞
    const wtx = Math.floor((this.cx + dir * (this.w / 2) + 2 * dir) / TILE);
    const hty = Math.floor((this.cy + this.gravDir * (-this.h / 2) + 2 * this.gravDir) / TILE);
    const solidOrSloped = (tx: number, ty: number): boolean =>
      st.inBounds(tx, ty) && !!st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty)] && st.isSolid(tx, ty);
    return solidOrSloped(wtx, hty) || solidOrSloped(wtx, Math.floor(this.cy / TILE));
  }
  /** 冲刺：双击窗口（dashTime ±15）/ 方向 / dashDelay 三态（0 就绪 / -1 滑行 / >0 冷却） */

  dashDir = 1;

  eocDash = 0;
  eocHitId = -1;
  prevInputX = 0;
  /** 水面行走状态（水上漂靴族：站在液面，按 ↓ 下潜） */
  onWaterSurface = false;
  /** 所在沙漠区（Game 每帧同步 scene.zoneDesert） */

-0.001) vy = Math.min(vy, vMax);\n      }\n      p.vy = vy;\n    } else if (!noFatigue) {\n      // 非悬停帧态：正常重力（:3440）\n      p.vy += DEFAULT_GRAVITY * p.gravDir;\n    }\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  /** 起跳瞬间向上的 hover 触发（Player.cs:26416：vy==0 且 CanHover 且按上且松键边沿） */\n  tryStartHover(p: MountedPlayerView): void {\n    if (p.vy === 0 && this.canHover(p) && p.inputUp && !p.prevInputJump) {\n      p.vy = -(this.acceleration + DEFAULT_GRAVITY + 0.001);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 退出飞行帧态探测（Mount.cs TryBeginningFlight :4509-4528） */\n  tryBeginningFlight(p: MountedPlayerView, state: number): void {\n    if (this.frameState === state || (state !== FRAME_IN_AIR && state !== FRAME_FLYING)\n      || !this.canHover(p) || p.inputUp || p.inputDown || p.inputJump) return;\n    const st = p.store;\n    if (!st) return;\n    const ty = Math.floor((p.y + p.h + 1) / 16);\n    let blocked = false;\n    for (let tx = Math.floor(p.x / 16); tx <= Math.floor((p.x + p.w - 1) / 16); tx++) {\n      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty)] && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { blocked = true; break; }\n    }\n    if (blocked && this.hoverIgnoresFatigue()) p.y -= 0.001;\n  }\n\n  /** 落地帧态探测（Mount.cs TryLanding :4544-4558：无升降输入且下方 4px 无地面） */\n  tryLanding(p: MountedPlayerView): void {\n    if ((this.frameState === FRAME_FLYING || this.frameState === FRAME_IN_AIR)\n      && !p.inputUp && !p.inputDown && !p.inputJump) {\n      const st = p.store;\n      if (!st) return;\n      const ty = Math.floor((p.y + p.h + 4) / 16);\n      let grounded = false;\n      for (let tx = Math.floor(p.x / 16); tx <= Math.floor((p.x + p.w - 1) / 16); tx++) {\n        if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty)] && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { grounded = true; break; }\n      }\n      if (!grounded) this.updateFrame(p, this.intendedGroundedFrame(p), p.vx, p.vy);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 地面帧态意图（Mount.cs GetIntendedGroundedFrame :4529-4543） */\n  intendedGroundedFrame(p: MountedPlayerView): number {\n    const still = p.vx === 0;\n    if (still) return FRAME_STANDING;\n    return FRAME_RUNNING;\n  }\n\n  // ---- 帧状态机（Mount.cs UpdateFrame :3563-4490 的通用路径 + 常用族特例） ----\n  updateFrame(p: MountedPlayerView, state: number, vx: number, vy: number): void {\n    const d = this.data;\n    if (!d) return;\n    if (this.frameState !== state) {\n      this.frameState = state;\n      // UFO（7）恒空中帧；熔岩鲨浸水恒游泳帧（:3688/:3697-3710）\n      if (!(this.type === 7)) this.frameCounter = 0;\n    }\n    if (this.type === 7) state = FRAME_FLYING;\n    if (state !== FRAME_STANDING) this.idleTime = 0;\n    // 离地行走宽限（:3599-3607）\n    if (vy === 0) this.walkingGraceTime = d.walkingGraceTimeMax ?? 0;\n    else if (this.walkingGraceTime > 0) this.walkingGraceTime--;\n    if (p.inputDown && p.vy > 0) this.walkingGraceTime = 0;\n\n    switch (state) {\n      case FRAME_STANDING: {\n        // 待机帧（:4139-4226）：站立 delay 计数 + idle 段（rand(900,1500) 后播 idle 动画）\n        if ((d.idleFrameCount ?? 0) !== 0) {\n          if (this.idleTime === 0) {\n            this.idleTimeNext = 900 + Math.trunc(this.idleRand * 600);\n            if (this.type === 2) this.idleTimeNext = 600 + Math.trunc(this.idleRand * 300);\n            this.idleRand = Math.random();\n          }\n          this.idleTime++;\n        }\n        this.frameCounter += 1;\n        if ((d.idleFrameCount ?? 0) !== 0 && this.idleTime >= this.idleTimeNext) {\n          const delay = d.idleFrameDelay ?? 12;\n          const step = Math.trunc((this.idleTime - this.idleTimeNext) / delay);\n          if (step >= (d.idleFrameCount ?? 0)) {\n            if (d.idleFrameLoop) { this.idleTime = this.idleTimeNext; this.frame = d.idleFrameStart ?? 0; }\n            else { this.frameCounter = 0; this.frame = d.standingFrameStart ?? 0; this.idleTime = 0; }\n          } else {\n            this.frame = (d.idleFrameStart ?? 0) + step;\n          }\n        } else if (this.frameCounter > (d.standingFrameDelay ?? 12)) {\n          this.frameCounter -= d.standingFrameDelay ?? 12;\n          this.frame++;\n        }\n        if (this.frame < (d.standingFrameStart ?? 0)\n          || this.frame >= (d.standingFrameStart ?? 0) + Math.max(1, d.standingFrameCount ?? 1)) {\n          this.frame = d.standingFrameStart ?? 0;\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n      case FRAME_RUNNING: {\n        // 跑动帧速率 = 水平速度（:4228-4262）；海盗船/暗黑魔法书族取速度比例\n        let rate = Math.abs(vx);\n        if (this.type === 44) rate = Math.max(1, Math.abs(vx) * 0.25);\n        if (this.type === 48) rate = Math.max(0.5, Math.hypot(vx, vy) * 0.125);\n        if (this.type === 50) rate = Math.abs(vx) * 0.5;\n        this.frameCounter += rate;\n        if (this.frameCounter > (d.runningFrameDelay ?? 12)) {\n          this.frameCounter -= d.runningFrameDelay ?? 12;\n          this.frame++;\n        }\n        if (this.frame < (d.runningFrameStart ?? 0)\n          || this.frame >= (d.runningFrameStart ?? 0) + Math.max(1, d.runningFrameCount ?? 1)) {\n          this.frame = d.runningFrameStart ?? 0;\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n      case FRAME_FLYING: {\n        this.frameCounter += 1;\n        if (this.frameCounter > (d.flyingFrameDelay ?? 12)) {\n          this.frameCounter -= d.flyingFrameDelay ?? 12;\n          this.frame++;\n        }\n        if (this.frame < (d.flyingFrameStart ?? 0)\n          || this.frame >= (d.flyingFrameStart ?? 0) + Math.max(1, d.flyingFrameCount ?? 1)) {\n          this.frame = d.flyingFrameStart ?? 0;\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n      case FRAME_IN_AIR: {\n        this.frameCounter += 1;\n        if (this.frameCounter > (d.inAirFrameDelay ?? 12)) {\n          this.frameCounter -= d.inAirFrameDelay ?? 12;\n          this.frame++;\n        }\n        if (this.frame < (d.inAirFrameStart ?? 0)\n          || this.frame >= (d.inAirFrameStart ?? 0) + Math.max(1, d.inAirFrameCount ?? 1)) {\n          this.frame = d.inAirFrameStart ?? 0;\n        }\n        // 蜜蜂悬停副帧（翅膀层 :4412-4425：疲劳越低拍越快）\n        if (this.type === 5 && (d.fatigueMax ?? 0) > 0) {\n          const t = this.fatigue / this.fatigueMax;\n          this.frameExtraCounter += 6 - 4 * t;\n          if (this.frameExtraCounter > (d.flyingFrameDelay ?? 12)) {\n            this.frameExtra++;\n            this.frameExtraCounter -= d.flyingFrameDelay ?? 12;\n          }\n          if (this.frameExtra < (d.flyingFrameStart ?? 0)\n            || this.frameExtra >= (d.flyingFrameStart ?? 0) + Math.max(1, d.flyingFrameCount ?? 1)) {\n            this.frameExtra = d.flyingFrameStart ?? 0;\n          }\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n      case FRAME_SWIMMING: {\n        // 游泳帧速率 = 平均速度（:4427-4446）\n        const rate = Math.trunc((Math.abs(vx) + Math.abs(vy)) / 2);\n        this.frameCounter += rate;\n        if (this.frameCounter > (d.swimFrameDelay ?? 12)) {\n          this.frameCounter -= d.swimFrameDelay ?? 12;\n          this.frame++;\n        }\n        if (this.frame < (d.swimFrameStart ?? 0)\n          || this.frame >= (d.swimFrameStart ?? 0) + Math.max(1, d.swimFrameCount ?? 1)) {\n          this.frame = d.swimFrameStart ?? 0;\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n      case FRAME_DASHING: {\n        this.frameCounter += Math.abs(vx);\n        if (this.frameCounter > (d.dashingFrameDelay ?? 40)) {\n          this.frameCounter -= d.dashingFrameDelay ?? 40;\n          this.frame++;\n        }\n        if (this.frame < (d.dashingFrameStart ?? 0)\n          || this.frame >= (d.dashingFrameStart ?? 0) + Math.max(1, d.dashingFrameCount ?? 1)) {\n          this.frame = d.dashingFrameStart ?? 0;\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 每帧帧态调度（Player.cs:35745-35790 的 mount.UpdateFrame 分派 1:1） */\n  updateFrameDispatch(p: MountedPlayerView): void {\n    if (p.vy !== 0 && this.walkingGraceTime <= 0) {\n      if (this.flyTime > 0 && !p.inputJump && p.inputJump === false && p.prevInputJump === false\n        && !this.canHover(p) && this.canFly(p)) {\n        // 注意：原版门 = FlyTime>0 && jump==0 && controlJump && !CanHover（飞行中）——\n        // 飞行帧态仅在按住跳跃时进入\n      }\n      if (this.flyTime > 0 && p.inputJump && !this.canHover(p)) {\n        this.tryBeginningFlight(p, FRAME_FLYING);\n        this.updateFrame(p, FRAME_FLYING, p.vx, p.vy);\n        this.tryLanding(p);\n      } else if (p.inWater) {\n        this.updateFrame(p, FRAME_SWIMMING, p.vx, p.vy);\n      } else {\n        this.tryBeginningFlight(p, FRAME_IN_AIR);\n        this.updateFrame(p, FRAME_IN_AIR, p.vx, p.vy);\n        this.tryLanding(p);\n      }\n    } else {\n      this.updateFrame(p, this.intendedGroundedFrame(p), p.vx, p.vy);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // ---- 上下坐骑 ----\n\n  /** 上坐骑（Mount.cs SetMount :6184-6296）：同型/无效    return true;\n  }\n\n  /** 下坐空间检查（Mount.cs CanDismountWithResult :6600：42 高玩家盒能放下才许下） */\n  canDismount(p: MountedPlayerView): boolean {\n    const st = p.store;\n    if (!st) return true;\n    const h = 42;\n    for (let tx = Math.floor(p.x / 16); tx <= Math.floor((p.x + p.w - 1) / 16); tx++) {\n      for (let ty = Math.floor((p.y + p.h - h) / 16); ty <= Math.floor((p.y + p.h - 1) / 16); ty++) {\n        if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty)] && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) return false;\n      }\n    }\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  tryDismount(p: MountedPlayerView): boolean {\n    if (!this.canDismount(p)) return false;\n    this.dismount(p);\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  dismount(p?: MountedPlayerView, game?: {\n    spawnParticles?(x: number, y: number, color: string, n: number, spd?: number): void;\n  }): void {\n    if (!this.active) return;\n    if (game?.spawnParticles && this.data?.spawnDust) {\n      game.spawnParticles((p?.x ?? 0) + (p?.w ?? 0) / 2, (p?.y ?? 0) + (p?.h ?? 0) / 2, '#C8C8C8', 10, 1.5);\n    }\n    this.reset();\n  }\n\n  reset(): void {\n    this.active = false;\n    this.type = -1;\n    this.frame = 0;\n    this.frameCounter = 0;\n    this.frameExtra = 0;\n    this.frameExtraCounter = 0;\n    this.frameState = FRAME_STANDING;\n    this.flyTime = 0;\n    this.fatigue = 0;\n    this.fatigueMax = 0;\n    this.idleTime = 0;\n    this.idleTimeNext = -1;\n    this.abilityCharging = false;\n    this.abilityCharge = 0;\n    this.abilityCooldown = 0;\n    this.abilityDuration = 0;\n    this.walkingGraceTime = 0;\n  }\n\n  /** 每 tick }\n}\n\n/** 物品 id（vanilla）→ 坐骑 id；非召唤物返回 -1 */\nexport function mountOfItem(vanillaItemId: number): number {\n  const m = ITEM_TO_MOUNT[String(vanillaItemId)];\n  return m === undefined ? -1 : let mountFlight = false;
if (mnt) {
  if (mnt.canHover(this)) { mnt.hover(this); mountFlight = true; }
  else if (mnt.canFly(this) && this.inputJump && this.jumpHold === 0) {
    if (mnt.flight()) {
      // Player.cs:26652-26680：爬升 0.1/tick、上限 -jumpSpeed*1.5；下落反抽 5×
      let js = mJumpSpd; if (mnt.type === 50) js *= 0.5;
      if (this.vy > 0) this.vy -= 0.1 * 5;
      else if (this.vy > -js * 1.5) this.vy -= 0.1;
      if (this.vy < -js * 1.5) this.vy = -js * 1.5;
    } else {
      // 能量耗尽：重力/3 + 终端 maxFall/3（:26683-26696）
      this.vy += GRAVITY / 3 * gd;
      if (gd === 1 ? this.vy > MAX_FALL_SPEED/3 : this.vy < -MAX_FALL_SPEED/3) this.vy = MAX_FALL_SPEED/3*gd;
    }
    mountFlight = true;
  }
}
```
Then guard: wings section `if (!mountFlight) {...}` — that's a bigger restructure. Alternative: when mounted, set `wings = 0` effectively and skip gravity. Let me see the structure: after this flight block, the code goes into wing/rocket/dash/wallcling and then gravity at the end. The gravity lines:
```ts
let grav = this.buffs.slowFall ? GRAVITY/3 : GRAVITY;
if (gliding) {...}
this.vy = gd===1 ? Math.min(this.vy+grav, MAX_FALL_SPEED) : ...;
```
I can add: `if (mountFlight) grav = 0;` and clamp skip. Cleanest minimal edit:
```ts
if (mountFlight) {
  // 坐骑已接管 vy：跳过翅膀/火箭靴/重力段（原版 else-if 链独占）
  ...
}
```
But the code between is a long flat sequence, not easily skip-able without wrapping in if/else. Options: wrap by early structure — set `grav = 0` and guard wing usage with `!mnt`. The wing section uses `wings>0`, `eq.wing`, `eq.rocketBoots` — while mounted vanilla zeroes wings/rocketBoots (mount.Active → rocketBoots=0, wings=0 unless CanUseWings). So guarding with mnt is semantically right:

- `const wings = (eq.wing && !mnt) ? eq.wing.logic : 0;`
- rocket section: guarded by rocketBoots != 0 — add `&& !mnt`.
- extra jumps: `if (!mnt || !mnt.blockExtraJumps)` — vanilla BlockExtraJumps blocks extra jumps (most mounts set it).
- gravity: `if (mountFlight) grav = 0`.

Let me apply these edits one by one. First insert the mount flight block right before the wing section comment.
````

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T06:31:19.668Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
The rocket/carpet sections are inside `} else {` (not onGround). To make mounts skip them, I'll guard the rocket boot condition with `!mnt`. The `flying` is already false since wings=0 when mounted. The carpet section: eq.carpet && ... — add !mnt. Simpler: since `flying` false and wings 0, the rocket branch requires `eq.rocketBoots > 0 && ...` — add `&& !mnt`. The carpet requires `eq.carpet` — add `&& !mnt`.

Then gravity: `let grav = ...; if (mountFlight) grav = 0;` — and also for hover mounts on the ground with hover active (not flying state) Hover adds gravity itself in the non-flying branch. Actually in my hover implementation, when frameState isn't flying/swim, it adds DEFAULT_GRAVITY (matching vanilla :3440). If our Player gravity also applies, we'd double. So: when `mnt && mnt.canHover(this)` is true (hover active regardless of state), the mount owns vy entirely → skip player gravity. mountFlight covers that since I set mountFlight=true for all hover cases.

Let me make these edits.
```

But wait: the turtle's frameState must be SWIMMING while in water for runSpeed→swimSpeed. updateFrameDispatch handles that (p.inWater → state 4). But the turtle has no hover; when in water and vy==0, it goes to intendedGroundedFrame (standing/running) → frameState not 4 → swimSpeed not applied. Hmm. In vanilla, the turtle in water: UpdateFrame(4) is only called when vy != 0 (the dispatch requires velocity.Y != 0 && walkingGrace<=0). When resting on the bottom in water, vy==0 → grounded frames, and RunSpeed returns runSpeed (2) — that matches vanilla exactly. OK, 1:1.

 "new_string": "  /** 坐骑召唤（Player.cs:42095：item.mountType != -1 且 flag2 → mount.SetMount）。\n   *  召唤物不消耗；再召同型 = 下坐（Player.cs:5768 TryToDismount 链） */\n  private useMountItem(vanillaItemId: number): boolean {\n    const mountId = mountOfItem(vanillaItemId);\n    if (mountId < 0) return false;\n    const p = this.player;\n    // 矿车族召唤物仍走轨道上车链（Minecart.ts 已接 MountID.Cart 路径，不回退）\n    if (isCartMount(mountId)) { this.tryQuickMinecart(); return true; }\n    if (p.ridingMount) {\n      // 已骑乘：同型下坐；异型换骑（SetMount :6194-6204 先 ClearBuff 再上）\n      if (p.mount.type === mountId) { this.tryDismountMount(); return true; }\n    }\n    if (p.ridingCart) return true;                    // 车上换坐骑需先下车（QuickMount 语义）\n    if (!p.mount.setMount(mountId, p, this)) return false;\n    // 起坐音效（Item.UseSound：鞍族 Item_25 / 胡萝卜 Item_79 / 猪龙 Item_80 / 史莱姆 Item_81）\n    const snd: Record<number, string> = {\n      0: 'Item_25', 1: 'Item_79', 2: 'Item_80', 3: 'Item_81', 4: 'Item_25', 5: 'Item_8',\n    };\n    this.playSfxFile(snd[mountId] ?? 'Item_25', 0.8, p.x, p.y);\n    p.useTime = 20;                                   // useAnimation 20（Item.cs 鞍族）\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  /** 下坐（Mount.TryDismount :6124：空间不足失败 → DoFailedDismountDust 尘 10） */\n  private tryDismountMount(): void {\n    const p = this.player;\n    if (!p.ridingMount) return;\n    if (!p.mount.tryDismount(p)) {\n      this.spawnParticles(p.cx, p.cy, '#C8C8C8', 10, 1.2);\n      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.MountDismountBlocked'));\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** QuickMinecart（Player.cs:5796-5827）：射程内取视线可达的最近轨道格上车 */"
}

// 原版文案查询门面。1:1 对照 Terarria1456/Terraria/Lang.cs 的便捷方法,
// 核心在 LanguageManager.ts(对照 LanguageManager.cs)。
//
// 数据 = public/l10n/<culture>.json(scripts/build-l10n.mjs 构建,扁平
// { [category]: { [key]: value } },全键 = category + '.' + key,已做英文兜底 overlay)。
// 例:Lang.text('UI.Play')、Lang.text('ItemName.HealingPotion')、Lang.text('LegacyWorldGen.9')
import { languageManager } from './LanguageManager';
import { ITEM_KEY_TO_ID, ITEM_NAME_BY_ID, NPC_NAME_BY_ID, BUFF_NAME_BY_ID, PROJECTILE_NAME_BY_ID, TILE_NAME_BY_ID, TILE_NAME_ITEM_BY_SHEET, TILE_NAME_ZH_BY_ID, TILE_NAME_EN_BY_ID, ITEM_NAME_ZH_BY_ID, ITEM_NAME_EN_BY_ID, WALL_NAME_ITEM_BY_WALL, WALL_NAME_ZH_BY_ID, WALL_NAME_EN_BY_ID } from './idNames.generated';
import { ITEM_BY_KEY, ITEM_DEFS } from '../data/items';
import { TILE_BY_KEY, TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
import { worldgenProgressKey, worldgenProgressStringKey } from './worldgenKeys';

export const Lang = {
  get loaded(): boolean { return languageManager.loaded; },
  /** 语言列表(设置面板数据源,来自 l10n/index.json) */
  get cultures() { return languageManager.cultures; },
  get activeCultureName(): string | null { return languageManager.activeCulture?.name ?? null; },
  get onChange() { return (fn: () => void) => languageManager.onChange(fn); },

  /** 启动初始化(默认 zh-Hans);load() 为旧签名兼容 */
  init(lang?: string | number): Promise<boolean> { return languageManager.init(lang); },
  async load(): Promise<boolean> { return Lang.init(); },

  /** 切换语言并广播(onLanguageChanged);失败返回 false 不改变状态 */
  setLanguage(culture: string | number): Promise<boolean> { return languageManager.setLanguage(culture); },

  /** 点路径取文案(全键 = category.key)。支持 {0}/{1} 占位符。缺失回退 key 本身并 warn 一次 */
  text(path: string, ...args: (string | number)[]): string {
    return languageManager.getTextValue(path, ...args);
  },

  /** Main.ValueToCoins（Main.cs:54649-54689 1:1）：铜币总值 →
   *  "N 铂金 N 金 N 银 N 铜"（非零档空格拼接，Currency.* 文案 toLowerCase，
   *  去尾空格；0 → 空串）。死亡掉钱行（Game.DroppedCoins {0}）与价格显示用 */
  valueToCoins(value: number): string {
    let rest = Math.max(0, Math.floor(value));
    let plat = 0, gold = 0, silver = 0;
    while (rest >= 1000000) { rest -= 1000000; plat++; }
    while (rest >= 10000) { rest -= 10000; gold++; }
    while (rest >= 100) { rest -= 100; silver++; }
    const copper = rest;
    let text = '';
    if (plat > 0) text += `${plat} ${Lang.text('Currency.Platinum').toLowerCase()} `;
    if (gold > 0) text += `${gold} ${Lang.text('Currency.Gold').toLowerCase()} `;
    if (silver > 0) text += `${silver} ${Lang.text('Currency.Silver').toLowerCase()} `;
    if (copper > 0) text += `${copper} ${Lang.text('Currency.Copper').toLowerCase()} `;
    if (text.length > 0) text = text.slice(0, -1);
    return text;
  },

  has(path: string): boolean { return languageManager.exists(path); },

  /** 聊天池随机一条（XxxChatter 类目；原版 Language.SelectRandom(Lang.CreateDialogFilter)） */
  chatter(category: string): string | null { return languageManager.randomFromCategory(category); },

  /** CreateDialogFilter 前缀随机（NPC.HasSpecialEventText 的 "XxxSpecialText.Yyy" 族；
   *  原版 Language.SelectRandom(Lang.CreateDialogFilter(prefix))，Lang.cs:425-428） */
  specialText(prefix: string): string | null { return languageManager.randomFromPrefix(prefix); },

  // ---- Legacy 数组等价物(Lang.cs:475-551 InitializeLegacyLocalization)----

 "new_string": "    void world;\n  }\n\n  /** 坐骑贴图叠画（Mount.Draw :5500-5560 主体 1:1）。\n   *  锚点 = 玩家底心 + (XOffset×dir, -(42+heightBoost)/2 + YOffset + PlayerOffset)\n   *  （原版 Position + (width/2 + XOffset, height/2 + YOffset + PlayerOffset)，height 为\n   *  mounted 盒 42+heightBoost、bottom 锚定——本仓玩家盒恒 42，按底心换算）；\n   *  源矩形 = (0, 帧高×frame, 全宽, 帧高)，origin = (宽/2, 帧高/2)，朝向翻转随 facing。\n   *  贴图不可得坐骑（MOUNT_TEXTURE_OK=false：马匹/滚轴鞋/Chillet 等 Extra 表缺）→\n   *  色块近似（胶囊体）并保留能力生效——登记于 vanilla-mounts.json textureOk */\n  private drawMountLayer(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, p: Player,\n    mnt: import('../entities/Mounts').MountInstance, layer: 'back' | 'front') {\n    const tex = mnt.textures;\n    const main = layer === 'back' ? tex.back : tex.front;\n    const extra = layer === 'back' ? tex.backExtra : tex.frontExtra;\n    if (this.atlas && main) {\n      const img = this.atlas.ensureVImage(main);\n      if (img) {\n        const d = mnt.data!;\n        const frames = Math.max(1, d.totalFrames ?? 1);\n        const fh = Math.floor(img.height / frames);\n        const fr = Math.min(frames - 1, Math.max(0, mnt.frame));\n        const xOff = (d.xOffset ?? 0) * (p.facing < 0 ? -1 : 1);\n        const anchorX = p.x + p.w / 2 + xOff;\n        const anchorY = p.y + p.h - (42 + (d.heightBoost ?? 0)) / 2 + (d.yOffset ?? 0) + mnt.playerOffset;\n        ctx.save();\n        ctx.translate(anchorX, anchorY);\n        if (p.facing < 0) ctx.scale(-1, 1);\n        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, fr * fh, img.width, fh, -img.width / 2, -fh / 2, img.width, fh);\n        // 副层（蜜蜂翅膀 Mount_BeeWings / Scutlix 眼族）：同锚错位 1px 叠画\n        if (extra) {\n          const ex = this.atlas.ensureVImage(extra);\n          if (ex) {\n            const efh = Math.floor(ex.height / frames);\n            const efr = layer === 'front' ? fr : Math.min(frames - 1, Math.max(0, mnt.frameExtra));\n            ctx.drawImage(ex, 0, efr * efh, ex.width, efh, -ex.width / 2 - 1, -efh / 2 - 1, ex.width, efh);\n          }\n        }\n        ctx.restore();\n        return;\n      }\n    }\n    // 色块近似：贴图缺表的坐骑（约 32/64）——胶囊体 + 朝向小三角头\n    if (layer === 'back') return;    // 近似体单层即可，避免双层叠深\n    const d = mnt.data!;\n    const bodyH = 42 + (d.heightBoost ?? 0) - 20;\n    const bodyW = Math.max(24, Math.min(56, bodyH * 1.3));\n    const cx = p.x + p.w / 2, cy = p.y + p.h - bodyH / 2 + mnt.playerOffset;\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.fillStyle = mountFallbackColor(mnt.type);\n    ctx.globalAlpha *= 0.9;\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.ellipse(cx, cy, bodyW / 2, bodyH / 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n    ctx.fill();\n    // 头部（朝向前伸）\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.ellipse(cx + p.facing * bodyW * 0.45, cy - bodyH * 0.25, bodyW * 0.16, bodyH * 0.16, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n    ctx.fill();\n    ctx.restore();\n  }"
}

```
Issues:
1. MOUNT_SETS.Cart undefined — my Sets regex must have failed. The regex: `/^\tpublic static (bool|int)\[\] (\w+) = Factory\.Create(?:Bool|Int)Set\(([^;]*)\);$/gm`. In MountID.cs the lines start with a tab. Let me check whether `CreateBoolSet(54, 57...)` has args matching /-?\d+/g — args are "54, 57, 58, 59, 60" → all numeric, def=false, pairs (54,57),(58,59),(60,undefined) — WRONG! For bool sets there are no key/value pairs — the args are the indices themselves. My pairing logic assumes int-set pairs. Fix: for bool sets, all args are indices set to true.

2. isCart false for mount 6 — because MOUNT_DATA['6'].Minecart is true but isCartMount uses MOUNT_SETS.Cart. Fixing #1 fixes this.

3. fatigueRecovery(5→3→1): vanilla: if fatigue > 2 → -2 else 0. So 3→1, not 0. My test expectation was wrong (5→3→1). Vanilla 1 → not >2 → 0. So third call gives 0. Test bug: I called twice expecting 0; need three calls. Fix test.

4. Hover test: after flyTime=2 consumed... let me trace: flyTime=2; hover#1 → flyTime-- = 1; hover#2 → 0 and fatigue += 1? My impl: `if (this.flyTime > 0) this.flyTime--; else if (...) fatigue += 1;` → after two hovers flyTime = 0, fatigue still 0 (the else-if only runs when flyTime was 0 at entry). Test expected fatigue 1 after second hover. Vanilla same semantics (`if (_flyTime > 0) _flyTime--; else if (...) _fatigue += num2;`). So fatigue becomes 1 only on the third hover. Fix test (three hovers).

5. UFO frameState forced FLYING: my updateFrame does `if (this.type === 7) state = FRAME_FLYING;` but frameState was already set to FRAME_STANDING before the override, and the switch uses `state` — frameState stays 0. Vanilla: case 7 sets `state = 2` (flying=3? no). Wait vanilla line: `case 7: state = 2; break;` — state 2 = FrameInAir! Not flying. Hmm: in Mount.cs UpdateFrame switch, `case 7: state = 2; break;` — so UFO always uses inAir frames (2). But frameState was already assigned at the top of UpdateFrame (`_frameState = state` with the original value). So vanilla's frameState for UFO = whatever was passed, while the frame animation uses inAir frames. My recollection of "UFO 恒飞行" was wrong — it's 恒 inAir frames (8 frames all states use frame group 0-8 anyway since all groups are 8/4/0).

So fix: in updateFrame, for type 7 force `state = FRAME_IN_AIR` (2) for the frame-group switch only, not frameState. And remove the frameState override. Update test to check the frame group behavior instead: e.g. ufo.updateFrame(view(), FRAME_STANDING, 0, 0) → frameState stays STANDING.

Hmm wait, but then in updateFrameDispatch for UFO: vy!=0 → canHover true → ... dispatch: `if (flyTime > 0 && inputJump && !canHover)` false (canHover true) → `else if (p.inWater)` no → `else tryBeginningFlight(2); updateFrame(2); tryLanding()`. tryBeginningFlight returns early for hover mounts (canHover → return). So UFO gets state 2 frames. On ground (vy==0) → intendedGroundedFrame → standing/running frames; UFO's standing group = 8 frames delay 4 — fine.

6. Slime constantJump test failed: p.y = 1238 > 1232 (77*16=1232). The player walked and fell off? mkPlayer at (30*16, 77*16) with floor at y=80 → 77*16=1232, player h=42 → bottom = 1274 > 80*16=1280? No: bottom = 1232+42 = 1274 < 1280. So the player falls to the floor: floor top at 1280, player bottom rests at 1280 → y = 1238. So after settling, p.y = 1238, not 1232. My assertion `p.y <= 77*16` is wrong. The auto-jump check should be: with inputX and onGround, the slime repeatedly jumps — verify by checking that p.y decreased below the resting 1238 at some point, or simpler: verify jump occurred via jumpHold>0 or vy<0 at some tick. Let me capture max height (min y) during the ticks.

Also note the player walks right and might fall off the platform edge? Platform spans x=20..60; player at x=480 (tile 30) walking right 30 ticks at ~4px/t = 120px → stays on platform. Good.

Let me fix the extractor's bool-set parsing, the Mounts.ts UFO frame override, and the tests.

  g.world.flags['downedGoblins'] = false;

  // ---- ④ 弹体端到端：PowderProj(10) 飞过第二只 534 → 转化 ----
  const { PowderProj } = await import('/src/entities/WeaponProj.ts');
  const en2 = Enemy.fromVanilla(534, g.player.cx + 200, g.player.cy - 40);
  g.entities.add(en2, 'enemies');
  const taxBefore = g.entities.npcs.filter(n => n.npcKey === 'tax_collector').length;
  const proj = new PowderProj(g.player.cx + 40, en2.cy - 24, 6, 0, 10);  // proj 10 净化粉，向 +x 飞
  g.entities.add(proj, 'projectiles');
  for (let i = 0; i < 240; i++) {   // 4 秒飞行（寿命 180t 内必穿过）
    proj.fixedUpdate(1, g.hooksProxy ?? g);
    if (en2.dead) break;
  }
  const taxAfter = g.entities.npcs.filter(n => !n.dead && n.npcKey === 'tax_collector').length;
  ok('④ 真弹体飞过 → 第二只 534 转化(桶+1)', en2.dead && taxAfter === taxBefore + 1);

  // ---- ⑤ 存档快照：bound TownNPC 保 bound 与位置（townNpcsForSave → serialize.npcs） ----
  const npcsSec = g.townNpcsForSave();
  const wizSave = npcsSec.find(n => n.key === 'wizard');
  const mechSave = npcsSec.find(n => n.key === 'mechanic');
  ok('⑤ 存档快照含 bound wizard/mechanic', !!wizSave && wizSave.bound === true && !!mechSave && mechSave.bound === true);
  ok('⑤ 解救后的哥布林无 bound 标', npcsSec.find(n => n.key === 'goblin_tinkerer')?.bound !== true);

  // ---- ⑥ 579 酒保链（downedBoss2 门 + 1/80）----
  const { TownNPC } = await import('/src/entities/TownNPC.ts');
  g.world.flags['downed_13'] = true;
  delete g.world.flags['savedBartender'];
  let hit579 = 0;
  for (let k = 0; k < 4000; k++) {
    const e = vs.spawn(g.player.cx, g.player.cy, new RNG(k * 7919 + 7), 30, 18);
    if (e && e.vanillaId === 579) hit579++;
  }
  ok('⑥ spawner 579 酒保链命中', hit579 > 0);

st.setTile(ctx, cty - 1, 0);\n  await sleep(100);\n  ok('⑤b 解封后可下坐', p.mount.tryDismount(p) === true && p.ridingMount === false);\n\n  // ---- ② 陆行模：蜥蜴 runSpeed 8 ----\n  holdItem('vi_3771_ancient_horn');\n  await useHeld();\n  ok('② 蜥蜴上坐(14)', p.mount.type === 14, `type=${p.mount.type}`);\n  g.input.keys.add('KeyD');\n  await sleep(900);\n  const vxLand = p.vx;\n  g.input.keys.delete('KeyD');\n  await sleep(150);\n  ok('② 蜥蜴加速 |vx|>4 且 ≤8.5（mount.RunSpeed 限速）', Math.abs(vxLand) > 4 && Math.abs(vxLand) <= 8.5, `vx=${vxLand.toFixed(2)}`);\n  // ---- ⑥ 召唤物再召同型 = 下坐 ----\n  await useHeld();\n  ok('⑥ 再召同型 → 下坐', p.ridingMount === false);\n\n  // ---- ③ 能量模：UFO hover ----\n  holdItem('vi_2769_cosmic_car_key');\n  await useHeld();\n  ok('③ UFO 上坐(7)', p.mount.type === 7, `type=${p.mount.type}`);\n  ok('③ UFO hover 能量池 320/320', p.mount.flyTime === 320, `flyTime=${p.mount.flyTime}`);\n  g.input.keys.add('Space');\n  await sleep(700);\n  const flyMid = p.mount.flyTime;\n  g.input.keys.delete('Space');\n  await sleep(250);\n  ok('③ 按跳消耗 flyTime（<320）', flyMid < 320, `flyTime=${flyMid}`);\n  ok('③ 悬停期间免摔标记', p.fallStartY === null || p.mount.frameState >= 2, `fs=${p.mount.frameState}`);\n  // 下坐收尾\n  g.input.keys.add('KeyR');\n  await sleep(160);\n  g.input.keys.delete('KeyR');\n  await sleep(200);\n  ok('③ UFO R 下坐', p.ridingMount === false);\n\n  // ---- ⑦ 矿车族召唤物走 Minecart 路径 ----\n  const { isCartMount, mountOfItem } = await import('/src/entities/Mounts.ts');\n  ok('⑦ 2343 矿车召唤物 isCartMount', isCartMount(mountOfItem(2343)) === true);\n  ok('⑦ 骑乘非矿车后矿车标志为假', isCartMount(4) === false);\n  res.pageMountState = { ridingMount: p.ridingMount, type: p.mount.type };\n  return res;\n});\nfor (const s of out.steps) console.log(s);\nconsole.log(`MOUNTS-PROBE ${out.steps.every(s => s.startsWith('PASS')) ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} ` +\n  `(${out.steps.filter(s => s.startsWith('PASS')).length}/${out.steps.length})`);\nconsole.log('pageErrors:', errs.slice(0, 3));\nawait browser.close();\nprocess.exit(out.steps.every(s => s.startsWith('PASS')) && errs.length === 0 ? 0 : 1);\n"
